Nintendo Switch

PCTL (Parental Controls) [2142-xxxx]

Display Language:
Error Code ? 2142-0100 ResultRestricted Use of this feature is restricted. Display the Auth pin authentication screen.
2142-0101 ResultFreeCommunicationRestricted Use of the unlimited communication with others feature is restricted. An error dialog box that is specific to errors related to Parental Controls is displayed.
2142-0102 ResultSnsPostRestricted Use of the social media posting feature is restricted. An error dialog box that is specific to errors related to Parental Controls is displayed.
2142-0103 ResultRestrictedByRating Use of this application is restricted because of its rating. An error dialog box that is specific to errors related to Parental Controls is displayed.
2142-0104 (TBD) Use of the xxxx (provisional) feature is restricted. An error dialog box that is specific to errors related to Parental Controls is displayed.
2142-0120 ResultWrongPinCode The entered unlock code is incorrect. Directly display the error in AuthUI.
2142-0130 ResultUnexpected Unexpected error. General-purpose error 2. An error has occurred.

If the problem persists, please refer to the Nintendo Support website.

2142-0180 ResultConfigurationNotAllowed Failed to change settings. Parental Controls cannot be changed with this software. No es posible modificar los ajustes del control parental con este programa. Les paramètres de contrôle parental ne peuvent pas être changés depuis ce logiciel. Não é possível alterar as configurações do controle dos pais com este software. Parental Controls settings cannot be changed using this software. Les paramètres de contrôle parental ne peuvent pas être modifiés depuis ce logiciel.
